Read JSON replacements\n    with open(DATA_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        replacements = json.load(f)\n    # Ensure all values are strings\n    replacements = {k: str(v) for k, v in replacements.items()}\n\n    # 2. Extract HWPX to temp directory\n    work_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"hwpx_work_\")\n    extracted_dir = os.path.join(work_dir, \"extracted\")\n    os.makedirs(extracted_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(TEMPLATE_PATH, \"r\") as zin:\n        zin.extractall(extracted_dir)\n\n    placeholder_pattern = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n    # 3. Process every XML file in the extracted archive\n    for root_dir, _dirs, files in os.walk(extracted_dir):\n        for fname in files:\n            if not fname.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                continue\n            file_path = os.path.join(root_dir, fname)\n\n            with open(file_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                original_text = f.read()\n\n            if \"{{\" not in original_text:\n                continue\n\n            tree = ET.parse(file_path)\n            xml_root = tree.getroot()\n\n            # Build parent map (child -> parent)\n            parent_map = {c: p for p in xml_root.iter() for c in p}\n\n            # Track <hp:p> paragraphs that were modified\n            modified_paras = set()\n\n            for t_elem in xml_root.iter(f\"{{{NS['hp']}}}t\"):\n                if t_elem.text is None:\n                    continue\n                original_t = t_elem.text\n                new_t = placeholder_pattern.sub(\n                    lambda m: replacements.get(m.group(1), m.group(0)),\n                    original_t,\n                )\n                if new_t != original_t:\n                    t_elem.text = new_t\n                    # Walk up to containing <hp:p>\n                    node = t_elem\n                    while node is not None and node.tag != f\"{{{NS['hp']}}}p\":\n                        node = parent_map.get(node)\n                    if node is not None:\n                        modified_paras.add(id(node))\n\n            # Remove linesegarray from modified paragraphs\n            for p_elem in xml_root.iter(f\"{{{NS['hp']}}}p\"):\n                if id(p_elem) in modified_paras:\n                    for child in list(p_elem):\n                        if child.tag == f\"{{{NS['hp']}}}linesegarray\":\n                            p_elem.remove(child)\n\n            tree.write(file_path, encoding=\"utf-8\", xml_declaration=True)\n\n    # 4. Re-package into output HWPX\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, \"w\", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n        for root_dir, _dirs, files in os.walk(extracted_dir):\n            for fname in files:\n                full_path = os.path.join(root_dir, fname)\n                arc_name = os.path.relpath(full_path, extracted_dir)\n                zout.write(full_path, arc_name)\n\n    # 5. Verify no placeholders remain\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT_PATH, \"r\") as zcheck:\n        for info in zcheck.infolist():\n            if info.filename.lower().endswith(\".xml\"):\n                content = zcheck.read(info.filename).decode(\"utf-8\")\n                if \"{{\" in content:\n                    raise RuntimeError(\n                        f\"Placeholder still remains in {info.filename}\"\n                    )\n\n    print(f\"Done. Output written to {OUTPUT_PATH}\")\n\n    # Cleanup\n    shutil.rmtree(work_dir, ignore_errors=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"model":"moonshotai/kimi-k2.5-0127","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"SiliconFlow","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2b208460-7767-434e-941a-a1818b6dd0fe","uuid":"89ae6488-f824-469f-ad82-85974b8920f5"}
